statement Refine.Carry.Cfg.mk.inj : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {w : Refine.Ask.World} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w_1 : Refine.Ask.World} {ctx_1 : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans_1 : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg_1 : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } = { w := w_1, ctx := ctx_1, ans := ans_1, bg := bg_1 } →
    w = w_1 ∧ ctx = ctx_1 ∧ ans = ans_1 ∧ bg = bg_1
statement Refine.Carry.Cfg.mk.injEq : ∀ {kind ord : Type} (w : Refine.Ask.World) (ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx)
  (ans : Refine.Ask.Answers) (bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord) (w_1 : Refine.Ask.World) (ctx_1 : SpecTwin.Ctx)
  (ans_1 : Refine.Ask.Answers) (bg_1 : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord),
  ({ w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } = { w := w_1, ctx := ctx_1, ans := ans_1, bg := bg_1 }) =
    (w = w_1 ∧ ctx = ctx_1 ∧ ans = ans_1 ∧ bg = bg_1)
statement Refine.Carry.Cfg.mk.sizeOf_spec : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : SizeOf kind] [inst_1 : SizeOf ord]
  (w : Refine.Ask.World) (ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx) (ans : Refine.Ask.Answers) (bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord),
  sizeOf { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } = 1 + sizeOf w + sizeOf ctx + sizeOf ans + sizeOf bg
statement Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.brecOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  (∀ (a_2 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) (t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_2),
      Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below t → motive a_2 t) →
    motive a_1 t

statement Refine.Carry.Meets.bridge : ∀ {m : Veil.Mode} {ρ σ α : Type} [Inhabited α] {act : Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α}
  {tr : Veil.Transition ρ σ} {assu : ρ → Prop} {pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ},
  Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post →
    act.toTransitionDerived = tr → ∀ {r : ρ} {s s' : σ}, assu r → pre r s → tr r s s' → post r s'

  {pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ},
  Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post → act.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming assu pre post
statement Refine.Carry.PlainInv.after_gate : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s →
    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
      (s.offered N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
        (s.retryArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
          (s.lrArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
            (s.promise N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
              (s.made N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true → s.gate N = true)

  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s →
    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true → s.got Q = true)
statement Refine.Carry.PlainInv.asked_offered : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s →
    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true) ∧
          (s.promise N Q = true → s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true)

  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]

      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
        s.askedParked N Q = true →
          s.got N = true ∨
            s.stop N = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true ∨ s.peerHas Q N = true ∨ ∃ J, Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q J

  Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s → ∀ (N : SpecTwin.Peer), s.inWindow N = true → s.retryArmed N = true
statement Refine.Carry.Run.brecOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1 → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1),
  (∀ (a_2 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) (t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_2),
      Refine.Carry.Run.below t → motive a_2 t) →
    motive a_1 t
statement Refine.Carry.abs_idle : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind]
  [inst_1 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_2 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_3 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord]
  {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root} {w : Refine.Ask.World} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers},
  Refine.Carry.IdleW ctx ans w n₀ k →
    ans.authenticable k.fst = true →
      (ans.installedBlock sl rt).isSome = true →
        Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord } =
          Refine.Carry.idleState kind ord n₀ (Refine.Ask.heldP w.st k) (Refine.Ask.stopP w.st k.fst)
            (Refine.Ask.retryArmedP w k) (Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st k)
statement Refine.Carry.abs_prom : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind]
  [inst_1 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_2 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_3 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord]
  {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root} {w : Refine.Ask.World} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers},
  Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises ctx ans w n₀ k →
    ans.authenticable k.fst = true →
      (ans.installedBlock sl rt).isSome = true →
        Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord } =
          Refine.Carry.promState kind ord n₀ (Refine.Ask.heldP w.st k) (Refine.Ask.stopP w.st k.fst)
            (Refine.Ask.retryArmedP w k) (Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st k) fun q => Refine.Ask.promisedP w.st q k
statement Refine.Carry.alpha_parkedWorld : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind]
  [inst_1 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_2 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_3 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord]
  {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer}
  {at_ : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {w : Refine.Ask.World} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk},
  w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
    ∀ (park prom : SpecTwin.Peer → Bool),
      (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now (Refine.Carry.parkedWorld i a p ctx w).st q k.fst = park q) →
        (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.promisedP (Refine.Carry.parkedWorld i a p ctx w).st q k = prom q) →
          Refine.Ask.alpha kind ord n₀ k ctx ans bg (Refine.Carry.parkedWorld i a p ctx w) =
            have __src := Refine.Ask.alpha kind ord n₀ k ctx ans bg w;
            { gate := __src.gate, installed := __src.installed, got := __src.got, stop := __src.stop,
              peerHas := __src.peerHas, offered := __src.offered, ledger := __src.ledger, inWindow := __src.inWindow,
              retryArmed := __src.retryArmed, lrArmed := __src.lrArmed,
              parked := fun n q => if n = n₀ then park q else bg.parked n q, askedParked := __src.askedParked,
              promise := fun n q => if n = n₀ then prom q else bg.promise n q, tied := __src.tied, made := __src.made,
              stage := __src.stage, everLastResort := __src.everLastResort, everEarly := __src.everEarly,
              everHeldBack := __src.everHeldBack, everTied := __src.everTied, everSwitched := __src.everSwitched,
              closedAsk := __src.closedAsk }
statement Refine.Carry.asks_opens_window : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} (sl : Nat) (rt : SpecTwin.Root)
  {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
  {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a) →
      Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = false →
        Refine.Ask.stopP w.st a.group = false →
          ans.expired a.group = false →
            decide (ctx.now - (w.st.askedAt.get? a).getD SpecTwin.negInf < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW) = false →
              Refine.Ask.freeP ctx ans w.st a ≠ [] →
                (∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → b ∈ w.st.askedAt → b = a) →
                  (∀ (xs : List SpecTwin.Peer) (b : SpecTwin.Key), (ans.ownOrder xs b).Perm xs) →
                    ctx.now - ctx.now < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW →
                      (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt
                              { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }).inWindow
                          n₀ =
                        true

statement Refine.Carry.bridge : ∀ {m : Veil.Mode} {ρ σ α : Type} [Inhabited α] (act : Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α)
  (tr : Veil.Transition ρ σ),
  act.toTransitionDerived = tr →
    ∀ (assu : ρ → Prop) (pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ),
      act.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming assu pre post →
        ∀ {r : ρ} {s s' : σ}, assu r → pre r s → tr r s s' → post r s'

  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord],
  Refine.Carry.FvRetryAsk kind ord →
    Refine.Carry.FvRetryNoop kind ord →
      ∀ {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root} {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord}
        {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
        Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c) →
          ¬Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c') →
            ¬Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'
statement Refine.Carry.offers_resolve_gives : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve r s →
    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
      ¬NoStallEquiv.work r s →
        ∀ {N Q : SpecTwin.Peer}, s.installed N = true → s.offered N Q = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true
statement Refine.Carry.offers_resolve_twin : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {c : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
  NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c) →
    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
      ¬NoStallEquiv.work r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c) →
        (c.ans.installedBlock sl rt).isSome = true →
          ∀ {q : SpecTwin.Peer},
            Refine.Ask.offeredP c.ans c.w.st k q = true →
              Refine.Ask.heldP c.w.st k = true ∨ Refine.Ask.stopP c.w.st k.fst = true
statement Refine.Carry.open_window_is_work : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] (r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord) {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord}
  {n : SpecTwin.Peer}, s.inWindow n = true → NoStallEquiv.work r s

      { gate := s.gate, installed := s.installed, got := s.got, stop := s.stop, peerHas := s.peerHas,

statement Refine.Carry.parkedP_of_park : ∀ {st st' : SpecTwin.State},
  st'.park = st.park →
    ∀ (now : Float) (q : SpecTwin.Peer) (g : SpecTwin.Group),
      Refine.Ask.parkedP now st' q g = Refine.Ask.parkedP now st q g
statement Refine.Carry.parkedP_park_other : ∀ {st : SpecTwin.State} {now t : Float} {p q : SpecTwin.Peer}
  {g g' : SpecTwin.Group},
  q ≠ p ∨ g' ≠ g →
    Refine.Ask.parkedP now
        { askedAt := st.askedAt, tried := st.tried, offers := st.offers, offered := st.offered, promises := st.promises,
          park := st.park.insert p ((st.park.getD p ∅).insert g t), askedParked := st.askedParked,
          lastResortArmed := st.lastResortArmed, ihaveFrames := st.ihaveFrames, ihaveAsked := st.ihaveAsked,
          heldBack := st.heldBack, received := st.received, mine := st.mine, state := st.state, tied := st.tied,
          queue := st.queue, pending := st.pending, pendingIds := st.pendingIds, tieSlot := st.tieSlot, owed := st.owed,
          owedAnswers := st.owedAnswers, sentTo := st.sentTo, slotHeld := st.slotHeld, paceQueue := st.paceQueue,
          stopPull := st.stopPull, peerHas := st.peerHas, holders := st.holders, serveCount := st.serveCount,
          origin := st.origin, planned := st.planned, pushed := st.pushed, desc := st.desc, inFlight := st.inFlight,
          waiters := st.waiters, sent := st.sent, unwanted := st.unwanted }
        q g' =
      Refine.Ask.parkedP now st q g'
statement Refine.Carry.parkedP_park_self : ∀ {st : SpecTwin.State} {now t : Float} {p : SpecTwin.Peer}
  {g : SpecTwin.Group},
  Refine.Ask.parkedP now
      { askedAt := st.askedAt, tried := st.tried, offers := st.offers, offered := st.offered, promises := st.promises,
        park := st.park.insert p ((st.park.getD p ∅).insert g t), askedParked := st.askedParked,
        lastResortArmed := st.lastResortArmed, ihaveFrames := st.ihaveFrames, ihaveAsked := st.ihaveAsked,
        heldBack := st.heldBack, received := st.received, mine := st.mine, state := st.state, tied := st.tied,
        queue := st.queue, pending := st.pending, pendingIds := st.pendingIds, tieSlot := st.tieSlot, owed := st.owed,
        owedAnswers := st.owedAnswers, sentTo := st.sentTo, slotHeld := st.slotHeld, paceQueue := st.paceQueue,
        stopPull := st.stopPull, peerHas := st.peerHas, holders := st.holders, serveCount := st.serveCount,
        origin := st.origin, planned := st.planned, pushed := st.pushed, desc := st.desc, inFlight := st.inFlight,
        waiters := st.waiters, sent := st.sent, unwanted := st.unwanted }
      p g =
    decide (now < t)
statement Refine.Carry.parkedWorld_parked_other : ∀ {i : Nat} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer}
  {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {w : Refine.Ask.World} {q : SpecTwin.Peer} {g : SpecTwin.Group},
  q ≠ p ∨ g ≠ a.group →
    Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now (Refine.Carry.parkedWorld i a p ctx w).st q g = Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now w.st q g
statement Refine.Carry.parkedWorld_parked_self : ∀ {i : Nat} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now (Refine.Carry.parkedWorld i a p ctx w).st p a.group =
    decide (ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL)

    (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord Refine.Carry.n0 Refine.Ask.kA.chunk 0 "r"

            Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'

  {k : SpecTwin.Chunk},
  Refine.Ask.promisedP
        { askedAt := st.askedAt, tried := st.tried, offers := st.offers, offered := st.offered,
          promises := st.promises.erase (a, p), park := st.park, askedParked := st.askedParked,
          lastResortArmed := st.lastResortArmed, ihaveFrames := st.ihaveFrames, ihaveAsked := st.ihaveAsked,
          heldBack := st.heldBack, received := st.received, mine := st.mine, state := st.state, tied := st.tied,
          queue := st.queue, pending := st.pending, pendingIds := st.pendingIds, tieSlot := st.tieSlot, owed := st.owed,
          owedAnswers := st.owedAnswers, sentTo := st.sentTo, slotHeld := st.slotHeld, paceQueue := st.paceQueue,
          stopPull := st.stopPull, peerHas := st.peerHas, holders := st.holders, serveCount := st.serveCount,
          origin := st.origin, planned := st.planned, pushed := st.pushed, desc := st.desc, inFlight := st.inFlight,
          waiters := st.waiters, sent := st.sent, unwanted := st.unwanted }
        q k =
      true ↔
    ∃ b, b.chunk = k ∧ (b, q) ≠ (a, p) ∧ (b, q) ∈ st.promises

    BridgeToy.raise.ext.tr (Refine.Carry.ToyTheory node) (Refine.Carry.ToyState node) node
        (BridgeToy.FieldAbstractType node) n r s s' →
      BridgeToy.flag_seen r s'

    (BridgeToy.Invariants (Refine.Carry.ToyTheory node) (Refine.Carry.ToyState node) node

statement Refine.Carry.witness_after_abs : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind]

statement Refine.Carry.witness_antecedent : ∀ (kind ord : Type) [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]

    ¬NoStallEquiv.work (Refine.Carry.rOn kind ord)
          (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord Refine.Carry.n0 Refine.Ask.kA.chunk 0 "r" (Refine.Carry.cWitnessAfter kind ord)) ∧
      (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord Refine.Carry.n0 Refine.Ask.kA.chunk 0 "r"
                (Refine.Carry.cWitnessAfter kind ord)).installed
            Refine.Carry.n0 =
          true ∧

                (Refine.Carry.cWitnessAfter kind ord)).offered
            Refine.Carry.n0 1 =
          true

definition Refine.Carry.AllOn : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Prop :=
fun kind ord r =>
  r.lastResort = true ∧
    r.owedAnswers = true ∧
      r.askLedger = true ∧ r.heldBackRetry = true ∧ r.rootTied = true ∧ r.noReask = true ∧ r.countsReset = true
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.ans : {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Ask.Answers :=
fun kind ord self => self.3
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.bg : {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord self => self.4
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.casesOn : {kind ord : Type} →
  {motive : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Sort u} →
    (t : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) →
      ((w : Refine.Ask.World) →
          (ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx) →
            (ans : Refine.Ask.Answers) →
              (bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord) → motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }) →
        motive t :=
fun {kind ord} {motive} t mk => Refine.Carry.Cfg.rec (fun w ctx ans bg => mk w ctx ans bg) t
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.ctorIdx : {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Nat :=
fun {kind ord} x => 0
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.ctx : {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → SpecTwin.Ctx :=
fun kind ord self => self.2
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.mk.noConfusion : {kind ord : Type} →
  {P : Sort u} →
    {w : Refine.Ask.World} →
      {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} →
        {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} →
          {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} →
            {w' : Refine.Ask.World} →
              {ctx' : SpecTwin.Ctx} →
                {ans' : Refine.Ask.Answers} →
                  {bg' : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} →
                    { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } = { w := w', ctx := ctx', ans := ans', bg := bg' } →
                      (w = w' → ctx = ctx' → ans = ans' → bg ≍ bg' → P) → P :=
fun {kind ord} {P} {w} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w'} {ctx'} {ans'} {bg'} eq k => id (Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusion ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ k)
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusion : {P : Sort u} →
  {kind ord : Type} →
    {t : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} →
      {kind' ord' : Type} →
        {t' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind' ord'} →
          kind = kind' → ord = ord' → t ≍ t' → Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusionType P t t' :=
fun {P} {kind ord} {t} {kind' ord'} {t'} eq_1 eq_2 eq_3 =>
  Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {kind'} =>
    {t' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind' ord'} → t ≍ t' → Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusionType P t t')
    (fun {t'} eq_3 =>
      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {ord'} =>
        {t' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord'} → t ≍ t' → Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusionType P t t')
        (fun {t'} eq_3 => ⋯ ▸ Refine.Carry.Cfg.casesOn t fun w ctx ans bg k => k ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯) eq_2 eq_3)
    eq_1 eq_3
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.noConfusionType : Sort u →
  {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → {kind' ord' : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind' ord' → Sort u :=
fun P {kind ord} t {kind' ord'} t' =>
  Refine.Carry.Cfg.casesOn t fun w ctx ans bg =>
    Refine.Carry.Cfg.casesOn t' fun w_1 ctx_1 ans_1 bg_1 => (w = w_1 → ctx = ctx_1 → ans = ans_1 → bg ≍ bg_1 → P) → P
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.recOn : {kind ord : Type} →
  {motive : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Sort u} →
    (t : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) →
      ((w : Refine.Ask.World) →
          (ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx) →
            (ans : Refine.Ask.Answers) →
              (bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord) → motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }) →
        motive t :=
fun {kind ord} {motive} t mk => Refine.Carry.Cfg.rec mk t
definition Refine.Carry.Cfg.w : {kind ord : Type} → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Ask.World :=
fun kind ord self => self.1
definition Refine.Carry.Covered.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {motive : (a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
      {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
      (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a)) (hheld : Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = false)
      (hstop : Refine.Ask.stopP w.st a.group = false) (hexp : ans.expired a.group = false)
      (hwin : decide (ctx.now - (w.st.askedAt.get? a).getD SpecTwin.negInf < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW) = false)
      (hfree : Refine.Ask.freeP ctx ans w.st a ≠ [])
      (hone : ∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → b ∈ w.st.askedAt → b = a)
      (hperm : ∀ (xs : List SpecTwin.Peer) (b : SpecTwin.Key), (ans.ownOrder xs b).Perm xs)
      (hW : ctx.now - ctx.now < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW),
      motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
        { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
    (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
        {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk ≠ k)
        (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a)) (hheld : Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = false)
        (hstop : Refine.Ask.stopP w.st a.group = false) (hexp : ans.expired a.group = false)
        (hwin : decide (ctx.now - (w.st.askedAt.get? a).getD SpecTwin.negInf < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW) = false)
        (hfree : Refine.Ask.freeP ctx ans w.st a ≠ []),
        motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
          { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
      (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
          {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
          (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a)) (hheld : Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = true)
          (hrest :
            Refine.Ask.retryArmedP { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
                a.chunk =
              true),
          motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
            { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
        (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
            {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
            (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a))
            (hheld : Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = true)
            (hlast :
              Refine.Ask.retryArmedP { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
                  a.chunk =
                false)
            (hclosed : Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st a.chunk = false),
            motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
              { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
          motive a a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {motive} {a a_1} t asks asksOther heldSame heldLast =>
  Refine.Carry.Covered.rec
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hheld hstop hexp hwin hfree hone hperm hW =>
      asks hk htimer hheld hstop hexp hwin hfree hone hperm hW)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hheld hstop hexp hwin hfree =>
      asksOther hk htimer hheld hstop hexp hwin hfree)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hheld hrest => heldSame hk htimer hheld hrest)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hheld hlast hclosed => heldLast hk htimer hheld hlast hclosed) t
definition Refine.Carry.Covered.recOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}

                  a.chunk =
                false)
            (hclosed : Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st a.chunk = false),
            motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
              { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
          motive a a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {motive} {a a_1} t asks asksOther heldSame heldLast =>
  Refine.Carry.Covered.rec asks asksOther heldSame heldLast t
definition Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} →
      (t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1) → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below t → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} {t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1}
  (t_1 : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below t),
  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k c c') (ih : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below hrun)
        (hrun_ih : motive c hrun), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      motive_1 t t_1 :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {a} {motive} {motive_1} {a_1} {t} t_1 start step =>
  Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.below.rec start (fun {c c'} hrun hstep ih hrun_ih ih_ih => step hrun hstep ih hrun_ih) t_1
definition Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  motive a ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k c c'), motive c' ⋯) →
      motive a_1 t :=

  Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.rec start (fun {c c'} hrun hstep hrun_ih => step hrun hstep) t
definition Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.recOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  motive a ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k c c'), motive c hrun → motive c' ⋯) →
      motive a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {a} {motive} {a_1} t start step => Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.rec start step t
definition Refine.Carry.FixtureFacts : Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.World → Bool → Prop :=
fun ans w held =>
  Refine.Ask.heldP w.st Refine.Ask.kA.chunk = held ∧
    Refine.Ask.retryArmedP w Refine.Ask.kA.chunk = true ∧
      Refine.Ask.retryArmedP { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx 0, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
            Refine.Ask.kA.chunk =
          false ∧
        Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st Refine.Ask.kA.chunk 1 = true ∧
          Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st Refine.Ask.kA.chunk Refine.Carry.n0 = false ∧
            decide (Refine.Ask.ctx1.now < SpecTwin.negInf) = false
definition Refine.Carry.FvInit.casesOn : {kind ord : Type} →

        [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] →
          [inst_4 : Inhabited ord] →
            [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              {motive : Refine.Carry.FvInit kind ord → Sort u} →
                (t : Refine.Carry.FvInit kind ord) →
                  ((offers_resolve : Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve rd st) →
                      (blocks : Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.blocks rd st) →
                        (offer_holder : Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offer_holder rd st) →
                          (ledger_before :
                              Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ledger_before rd st) →
                            (ask_holder : Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ask_holder rd st) →
                              (claims_true :
                                  Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.claims_true rd st) →
                                (window_retry :
                                    Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.window_retry rd st) →
                                  (askedparked_offered :
                                      Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                        NoStallEquiv.askedparked_offered rd st) →
                                    (askedparked_parked :
                                        Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                          NoStallEquiv.askedparked_parked rd st) →
                                      (after_gate :
                                          Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.after_gate rd st) →
                                        (made_order :
                                            Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                              NoStallEquiv.made_order rd st) →
                                          (stage_made :
                                              Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                NoStallEquiv.stage_made rd st) →
                                            (asked_offered :
                                                Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                  NoStallEquiv.asked_offered rd st) →
                                              (first_ask :
                                                  Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                    NoStallEquiv.first_ask rd st) →
                                                (second_ask :
                                                    Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                      NoStallEquiv.second_ask rd st) →
                                                  (two_before :
                                                      Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                        NoStallEquiv.two_before rd st) →
                                                    (third_answered :
                                                        Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                          NoStallEquiv.third_answered rd st) →

                                                        (parked_timer :

                                                            (asked_parked :

                                                                  NoStallEquiv.asked_parked rd st) →
                                                              motive ⋯) →
                    motive t :=

                                        NoStallEquiv.askedparked_offered rd st) →
                                    (askedparked_parked :
                                        Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                          NoStallEquiv.askedparked_parked rd st) →
                                      (after_gate :
                                          Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                            NoStallEquiv.after_gate rd st) →
                                        (made_order :
                                            Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                              NoStallEquiv.made_order rd st) →
                                          (stage_made :
                                              Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                NoStallEquiv.stage_made rd st) →
                                            (asked_offered :
                                                Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                  NoStallEquiv.asked_offered rd st) →
                                              (first_ask :
                                                  Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                    NoStallEquiv.first_ask rd st) →
                                                (second_ask :
                                                    Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                      NoStallEquiv.second_ask rd st) →
                                                  (two_before :
                                                      Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                        NoStallEquiv.two_before rd st) →
                                                    (third_answered :
                                                        Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                          NoStallEquiv.third_answered rd st) →
                                                      (unparked_retry :
                                                          Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                            NoStallEquiv.unparked_retry rd st) →
                                                        (parked_timer :
                                                            Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                              NoStallEquiv.parked_timer rd st) →
                                                          (offer_active :
                                                              Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                                NoStallEquiv.offer_active rd st) →
                                                            (asked_parked :
                                                                Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                                                  NoStallEquiv.asked_parked rd st) →
                                                              motive ⋯) →
                    motive t :=

definition Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises.casesOn : {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} →
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} →
    {w : Refine.Ask.World} →
      {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} →
        {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} →
          {motive : Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises ctx ans w n₀ k → Sort u} →
            (t : Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises ctx ans w n₀ k) →
              ((window : Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st k = false) →
                  (lr : w.st.lastResortArmed.contains k = false) →
                    (park : ∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now w.st q k.fst = false) →
                      (askedParked : ∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), (w.st.askedParked.getD k ∅).contains q = false) →
                        (tied : ∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.tiedP w.st q k = false) →
                          (queued : ∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.queuedP w.st q k = false) →
                            (made : ∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), w.made k q = 0) →
                              (self : Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st k n₀ = false) → motive ⋯) →
                motive t :=
fun {ctx} {ans} {w} {n₀} {k} {motive} t mk => mk ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯
