definition Refine.Carry.InitMeets : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop) → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] cl =>
  Refine.Carry.Meets NoStallEquiv.initializer.ext (Refine.Carry.MAssu kind ord) (fun x x_1 => True) cl
definition Refine.Carry.MAssu : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] → [Inhabited ord] → [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] =>
  NoStallEquiv.Assumptions (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord) SpecTwin.Peer kind ord
definition Refine.Carry.MChi : Type → Type → NoStallEquiv.State.Label → Type :=
fun kind ord => NoStallEquiv.FieldAbstractType SpecTwin.Peer kind ord
definition Refine.Carry.MInv : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] =>
  NoStallEquiv.Invariants (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord) (Refine.Ask.MState kind ord) SpecTwin.Peer kind ord
    (Refine.Carry.MChi kind ord)
definition Refine.Carry.Meets.casesOn : {m : Veil.Mode} →
  {ρ σ α : Type} →
    {act : Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α} →
      {assu : ρ → Prop} →
        {pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ} →
          {motive : Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post → Sort u} →
            (t : Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post) →
              ((out : act.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming assu pre post) → motive ⋯) → motive t :=
fun {m} {ρ σ α} {act} {assu} {pre post} {motive} t mk => mk ⋯
definition Refine.Carry.Meets.recOn : {m : Veil.Mode} →
  {ρ σ α : Type} →
    {act : Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α} →
      {assu : ρ → Prop} →
        {pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ} →
          {motive : Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post → Sort u} →
            (t : Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post) →
              ((out : act.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming assu pre post) → motive ⋯) → motive t :=
@Refine.Carry.Meets.casesOn
definition Refine.Carry.OutstandingP : (kind ord : Type) →
  [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Peer → Prop :=
fun kind ord [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] s n q =>
  (∃ J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ n q J = true) ∨ s.promise n q = true
definition Refine.Carry.ParkOtherMeets : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop) → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] cl =>
  ∀ (n q : SpecTwin.Peer),
    Refine.Carry.Meets (NoStallEquiv.parkOther.ext n q) (Refine.Carry.MAssu kind ord) (Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord) cl
definition Refine.Carry.PipelineP : (kind ord : Type) →
  [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Peer → ord → Prop :=
fun kind ord [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] s n q j =>
  s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ n q j = true ∨
    s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF n q j = true ∨
      s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ q n j = true ∨
        s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed q n j = true ∨ s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF q n j = true
definition Refine.Carry.PlainInv.casesOn : {kind ord : Type} →
  [inst : DecidableEq kind] →
    [inst_1 : Inhabited kind] →
      [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] →
          [inst_4 : Inhabited ord] →
            [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} →
                {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} →
                  {motive : Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s → Sort u} →
                    (t : Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s) →
                      ((offers_resolve :
                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                ¬NoStallEquiv.work r s →
                                  s.installed N = true → s.offered N Q = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                          (blocks :
                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                (s.installed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                  (s.got N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧
                                    (s.stop N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧
                                      (s.tied N Q = true → ¬s.installed N = true)) →
                            (offer_holder :
                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                  (s.offered N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                    (s.ledger N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧ (s.tied N Q = true → s.got Q = true)) →
                              (ledger_before : ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.ledger N Q = true → ¬s.gate N = true) →
                                (ask_holder :
                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true →
                                                s.got Q = true) ∧
                                              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true →
                                                s.got Q = true)) →
                                  (claims_true :
                                      ∀ (Q N : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                        s.peerHas Q N = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                                    (window_retry :
                                        ∀ (N : SpecTwin.Peer), s.inWindow N = true → s.retryArmed N = true) →
                                      (askedparked_offered :
                                          ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                            Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                              s.askedParked N Q = true → s.offered N Q = true) →
                                        (askedparked_parked :
                                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.askedParked N Q = true → s.parked N Q = true) →
                                          (after_gate :
                                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                (s.offered N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                  (s.retryArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                    (s.lrArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                      (s.promise N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                        (s.made N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                              s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true →
                                                                s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true →
                                                                  s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J =
                                                                      true →
                                                                    s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J =
                                                                      true →
                                                                    s.gate N = true)) →
                                            (made_order :
                                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                  (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                                                      s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true) ∧
                                                    (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                                        s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true) ∧
                                                      (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o4 = true →
                                                        s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true)) →
                                              (stage_made :
                                                  ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                        s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true →
                                                          s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true →
                                                            s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true →
                                                              s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true →
                                                              s.made N Q J = true)) →
                                                (asked_offered :
                                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                            s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true) ∧
                                                          (s.promise N Q = true →
                                                            s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true)) →
                                                  (first_ask :
                                                      ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                        Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                          ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                            s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true →
                                                              (∃ J,
                                                                  s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J =
                                                                    true) ∨
                                                                s.promise N Q = true ∨
                                                                  s.parked N Q = true ∨
                                                                    s.tied N Q = true ∨
                                                                      s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                                                    (second_ask :
                                                        ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                          Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                            ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                              s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                                                                s.askedParked N Q = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true) →
                                                      (two_before :
                                                          ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                            Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                              ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                                ¬s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true) →
                                                        (third_answered :
                                                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                                                  Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q
                                                                      NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 ∨
                                                                    s.got N = true ∨
                                                                      s.stop N = true ∨ s.peerHas Q N = true) →
                                                          (unparked_retry :
                                                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                  s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                    ¬s.parked N Q = true →
                                                                      ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                                        s.got N = true ∨
                                                                          s.stop N = true ∨ s.retryArmed N = true) →
                                                            (parked_timer :
                                                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                  Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                    s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                      s.parked N Q = true →
                                                                        ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                                          ¬s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                                            s.got N = true ∨
                                                                              s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                                s.retryArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                  s.lrArmed N = true) →
                                                              (offer_active :
                                                                  ∀ (N M : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                      s.installed N = true →
                                                                        s.offered N M = true →
                                                                          s.got N = true ∨
                                                                            s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                              s.retryArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                s.lrArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                  (∃ Q J,
                                                                                      Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s
                                                                                        N Q J) ∨
                                                                                    ∀ (Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                                      s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                                        s.parked N Q = true ∧
                                                                                          s.askedParked N Q = true) →
                                                                (asked_parked :
                                                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                        s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                                          s.got N = true ∨
                                                                            s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                              s.tied N Q = true ∨
                                                                                s.peerHas Q N = true ∨
                                                                                  ∃ J,
                                                                                    Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N
                                                                                      Q J) →
                                                                  motive ⋯) →
                        motive t :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r} {s} {motive} t mk =>
  mk ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ ⋯
definition Refine.Carry.PlainInv.recOn : {kind ord : Type} →
  [inst : DecidableEq kind] →
    [inst_1 : Inhabited kind] →
      [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] →
          [inst_4 : Inhabited ord] →
            [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} →
                {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} →
                  {motive : Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s → Sort u} →
                    (t : Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s) →
                      ((offers_resolve :
                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                ¬NoStallEquiv.work r s →
                                  s.installed N = true → s.offered N Q = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                          (blocks :
                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                (s.installed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                  (s.got N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧
                                    (s.stop N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧
                                      (s.tied N Q = true → ¬s.installed N = true)) →
                            (offer_holder :
                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                  (s.offered N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                    (s.ledger N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧ (s.tied N Q = true → s.got Q = true)) →
                              (ledger_before : ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.ledger N Q = true → ¬s.gate N = true) →
                                (ask_holder :
                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true →
                                                s.got Q = true) ∧
                                              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true →
                                                s.got Q = true)) →
                                  (claims_true :
                                      ∀ (Q N : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                        s.peerHas Q N = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                                    (window_retry :
                                        ∀ (N : SpecTwin.Peer), s.inWindow N = true → s.retryArmed N = true) →
                                      (askedparked_offered :
                                          ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                            Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                              s.askedParked N Q = true → s.offered N Q = true) →
                                        (askedparked_parked :
                                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.askedParked N Q = true → s.parked N Q = true) →
                                          (after_gate :
                                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                (s.offered N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                  (s.retryArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                    (s.lrArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                      (s.promise N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                        (s.made N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                              s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true →
                                                                s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true →
                                                                  s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J =
                                                                      true →
                                                                    s.gate N = true) ∧
                                                                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J =
                                                                      true →
                                                                    s.gate N = true)) →
                                            (made_order :
                                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                  (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                                                      s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true) ∧
                                                    (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                                        s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true) ∧
                                                      (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o4 = true →
                                                        s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true)) →
                                              (stage_made :
                                                  ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                        s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true →
                                                          s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true →
                                                            s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true →
                                                              s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true →
                                                              s.made N Q J = true)) →
                                                (asked_offered :
                                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                                                      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                                            s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true) ∧
                                                          (s.promise N Q = true →
                                                            s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true)) →
                                                  (first_ask :
                                                      ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                        Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                          ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                            s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true →
                                                              (∃ J,
                                                                  s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J =
                                                                    true) ∨
                                                                s.promise N Q = true ∨
                                                                  s.parked N Q = true ∨
                                                                    s.tied N Q = true ∨
                                                                      s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                                                    (second_ask :
                                                        ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                          Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                            ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                              s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                                                                s.askedParked N Q = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true) →
                                                      (two_before :
                                                          ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                            Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                              ¬s.installed N = true →
                                                                ¬s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true) →
                                                        (third_answered :
                                                            ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                                                  Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q
                                                                      NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 ∨
                                                                    s.got N = true ∨
                                                                      s.stop N = true ∨ s.peerHas Q N = true) →
                                                          (unparked_retry :
                                                              ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                  s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                    ¬s.parked N Q = true →
                                                                      ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                                        s.got N = true ∨
                                                                          s.stop N = true ∨ s.retryArmed N = true) →
                                                            (parked_timer :
                                                                ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                  Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                    s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                      s.parked N Q = true →
                                                                        ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                                          ¬s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                                            s.got N = true ∨
                                                                              s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                                s.retryArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                  s.lrArmed N = true) →
                                                              (offer_active :
                                                                  ∀ (N M : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                      s.installed N = true →
                                                                        s.offered N M = true →
                                                                          s.got N = true ∨
                                                                            s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                              s.retryArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                s.lrArmed N = true ∨
                                                                                  (∃ Q J,
                                                                                      Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s
                                                                                        N Q J) ∨
                                                                                    ∀ (Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                                      s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                                        s.parked N Q = true ∧
                                                                                          s.askedParked N Q = true) →
                                                                (asked_parked :
                                                                    ∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                                      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                                        s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                                          s.got N = true ∨
                                                                            s.stop N = true ∨
                                                                              s.tied N Q = true ∨
                                                                                s.peerHas Q N = true ∨
                                                                                  ∃ J,
                                                                                    Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N
                                                                                      Q J) →
                                                                  motive ⋯) →
                        motive t :=
@Refine.Carry.PlainInv.casesOn
definition Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {motive : (a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
      {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
      (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_)) (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = none),
      motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
        { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
    (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
        {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
        (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
        (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = true),
        motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
          { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
      (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
          {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
          (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
          (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false) (hg : a.group ≠ k.fst),
          motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
            { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
        (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
            {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
            (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
            (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
            (hone : ∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises → b = a)
            (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
            motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
              { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
          (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
              {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
              (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
              (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
              (hmore : ∃ b, b ≠ a ∧ b.chunk = a.chunk ∧ (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises)
              (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
              motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
            (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
                {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk ≠ k)
                (hg : a.group = k.fst) (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
                (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
                (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
                motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                  { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
              motive a a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {motive} {a a_1} t gone other otherGroup due more otherChunk =>
  Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.rec (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} htimer hget => gone htimer hget)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_ t} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} htimer hget hdue => other htimer hget hdue)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_ t} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} htimer hget hdue hg => otherGroup htimer hget hdue hg)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_ t} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hget hdue hone hnow =>
      due hk htimer hget hdue hone hnow)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_ t} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk htimer hget hdue hmore hnow =>
      more hk htimer hget hdue hmore hnow)
    (fun {i} {d} {a} {p} {at_ t} {ctx} {ans} {bg} {w} hk hg htimer hget hdue hnow =>
      otherChunk hk hg htimer hget hdue hnow)
    t
definition Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.recOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {motive : (a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k a a_1 → Prop}
  {a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k a a_1),
  (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
      {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
      (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_)) (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = none),
      motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
        { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
    (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
        {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
        (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
        (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = true),
        motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
          { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
      (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
          {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
          (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
          (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false) (hg : a.group ≠ k.fst),
          motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
            { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
        (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
            {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
            (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
            (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
            (hone : ∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises → b = a)
            (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
            motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
              { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
          (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
              {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk = k)
              (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
              (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
              (hmore : ∃ b, b ≠ a ∧ b.chunk = a.chunk ∧ (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises)
              (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
              motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
            (∀ {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
                {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World} (hk : a.chunk ≠ k)
                (hg : a.group = k.fst) (htimer : w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_))
                (hget : w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t) (hdue : (t != at_) = false)
                (hnow : ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL),
                motive { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                  { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg } ⋯) →
              motive a a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} {n₀} {k} {motive} {a a_1} t gone other otherGroup due more otherChunk =>
  Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.rec gone other otherGroup due more otherChunk t
definition Refine.Carry.PromiseDueMeets : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop) → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] cl =>
  ∀ (n q : SpecTwin.Peer),
    Refine.Carry.Meets (NoStallEquiv.promiseDue.ext n q) (Refine.Carry.MAssu kind ord) (Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord) cl
definition Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              (Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop) → Prop :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] cl =>
  ∀ (n q : SpecTwin.Peer) (j : ord) (full : Bool),
    Refine.Carry.Meets (NoStallEquiv.retryAsk.ext n q j full) (Refine.Carry.MAssu kind ord) (Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord)
      cl

    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →

definition Refine.Carry.Run.below.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} →
      (t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1) → Refine.Carry.Run.below t → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} {t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1} (t_1 : Refine.Carry.Run.below t),
  motive_1 ⋯ ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c') (ih : Refine.Carry.Run.below hrun)
        (hrun_ih : motive c hrun), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      motive_1 t t_1 :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀} {k} {sl} {rt} {r} {a} {motive} {motive_1} {a_1} {t} t_1 start step =>
  Refine.Carry.Run.below.rec start (fun {c c'} hrun hstep ih hrun_ih ih_ih => step hrun hstep ih hrun_ih) t_1
definition Refine.Carry.Run.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}

  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1),
  motive a ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'), motive c' ⋯) →
      motive a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀} {k} {sl} {rt} {r} {a} {motive} {a_1} t start step =>
  Refine.Carry.Run.rec start (fun {c c'} hrun hstep hrun_ih => step hrun hstep) t
definition Refine.Carry.Run.recOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : (a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord) → Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1 → Prop}
  {a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (t : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1),
  motive a ⋯ →
    (∀ {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord} (hrun : Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a c)
        (hstep : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'), motive c hrun → motive c' ⋯) →
      motive a_1 t :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀} {k} {sl} {rt} {r} {a} {motive} {a_1} t start step =>
  Refine.Carry.Run.rec start step t
definition Refine.Carry.Step.casesOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1 → Prop} (t : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1),
  (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k a a_1), motive ⋯) →
    (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a_1 = Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a), motive ⋯) →
      (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a_1)), motive ⋯) → motive t :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀} {k} {sl} {rt} {r} {a a_1} {motive} t covered same other =>
  Refine.Carry.Step.rec (fun h => covered h) (fun h => same h) (fun h => other h) t
definition Refine.Carry.Step.recOn : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {a a_1 : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord}
  {motive : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1 → Prop} (t : Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r a a_1),
  (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k a a_1), motive ⋯) →
    (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a_1 = Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a), motive ⋯) →
      (∀ (h : Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt a_1)), motive ⋯) → motive t :=
fun {kind ord} [DecidableEq kind] [Inhabited kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [Inhabited ord]
    [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀} {k} {sl} {rt} {r} {a a_1} {motive} t covered same other =>
  Refine.Carry.Step.rec covered same other t
definition Refine.Carry.ToyState : Type → Type :=
fun node => BridgeToy.State (BridgeToy.FieldAbstractType node)
definition Refine.Carry.ToyTheory : Type → Type :=
fun node => BridgeToy.Theory node
definition Refine.Carry.WorkP : (kind ord : Type) →
  [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop :=
fun kind ord [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] r s =>
  (∃ N Q J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true) ∨
    (∃ Q N J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true) ∨
      (∃ Q N J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true) ∨
        (∃ Q N J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true) ∨
          (∃ N Q J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true) ∨
            (∃ N, s.inWindow N = true) ∨
              (∃ N, s.retryArmed N = true ∧ ¬s.inWindow N = true ∧ (s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true)) ∨
                (∃ N Q,
                    s.retryArmed N = true ∧
                      ¬s.inWindow N = true ∧
                        ¬s.got N = true ∧
                          ¬s.stop N = true ∧
                            s.offered N Q = true ∧
                              ¬s.parked N Q = true ∧
                                ¬s.tied N Q = true ∧ ¬(r.noReask = true ∧ Refine.Carry.OutstandingP kind ord s N Q)) ∨
                  (∃ N,
                      s.retryArmed N = true ∧
                        ¬s.inWindow N = true ∧
                          ¬s.got N = true ∧
                            ¬s.stop N = true ∧
                              ∀ (Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.offered N Q = true → s.parked N Q = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true) ∨
                    (∃ N,
                        s.lrArmed N = true ∧
                          (s.got N = true ∨
                            s.stop N = true ∨
                              s.inWindow N = true ∨
                                ∀ (Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                  s.offered N Q = true →
                                    s.tied N Q = true ∨ s.parked N Q = true ∧ s.askedParked N Q = true)) ∨
                      (∃ N Q,
                          s.lrArmed N = true ∧
                            ¬s.got N = true ∧
                              ¬s.stop N = true ∧
                                ¬s.inWindow N = true ∧
                                  s.offered N Q = true ∧
                                    ¬s.tied N Q = true ∧
                                      ¬(r.noReask = true ∧ Refine.Carry.OutstandingP kind ord s N Q) ∧
                                        (¬s.parked N Q = true ∨
                                          (∀ (Q' : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                              s.offered N Q' = true → s.parked N Q' = true ∨ s.tied N Q' = true) ∧
                                            ¬s.askedParked N Q = true)) ∨
                        ∃ N Q, s.promise N Q = true
definition Refine.Carry.abs : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
      [DecidableEq ord] →
        [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
          SpecTwin.Peer →
            SpecTwin.Chunk → Nat → SpecTwin.Root → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord [DecidableEq kind] [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [DecidableEq ord] [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] n₀
    k sl rt c =>
  Refine.Ask.alpha kind ord n₀ k c.ctx c.ans (Refine.Carry.withBlocks kind ord n₀ k sl rt c.ans c.bg) c.w
definition Refine.Carry.ansBlock : Refine.Ask.Answers :=
{ connectedTopicPeers := [1], authenticable := fun x => true,
  installedBlock := fun x x_1 => some { slot := 0, groupId := none, desc := none } }
definition Refine.Carry.ansExpired : Refine.Ask.Answers :=
have __src := Refine.Carry.ansBlock;
{ expired := fun x => true, connectedTopicPeers := __src.connectedTopicPeers, ownOrder := __src.ownOrder,
  listed := __src.listed, admits := __src.admits, enqueue := __src.enqueue, knownId := __src.knownId,
  authenticable := __src.authenticable, shuffled := __src.shuffled, validHave := __src.validHave, seen := __src.seen,
  accepted := __src.accepted, live := __src.live, validateChunkNode := __src.validateChunkNode,
  queueOrder := __src.queueOrder, meshAnd := __src.meshAnd, installedBlock := __src.installedBlock,
  admitted := __src.admitted, reserve := __src.reserve, bidGroup := __src.bidGroup, pendingOrder := __src.pendingOrder,
  tieSlotOrder := __src.tieSlotOrder, tiedRootsOrder := __src.tiedRootsOrder, tiedPeersOrder := __src.tiedPeersOrder,
  tiedGroupsOrder := __src.tiedGroupsOrder, hasRoom := __src.hasRoom, popOwedAnswer := __src.popOwedAnswer,
  tiebreak := __src.tiebreak, peerIdBytes := __src.peerIdBytes, chunksGroups := __src.chunksGroups,
  chunkIndices := __src.chunkIndices, owedOrder := __src.owedOrder, sha256 := __src.sha256,
  serialize := __src.serialize, snappyBlockEncode := __src.snappyBlockEncode, padEach := __src.padEach,
  rsParity := __src.rsParity, merkleRoot := __src.merkleRoot, merkleProof := __src.merkleProof,
  contentId := __src.contentId, connected := __src.connected, groupBits := __src.groupBits,
  heldBackOrder := __src.heldBackOrder, gossipIds := __src.gossipIds, topicPeers := __src.topicPeers,
  meshHas := __src.meshHas, gossipEligible := __src.gossipEligible, sample := __src.sample, meshList := __src.meshList,
  resolve := __src.resolve, chunkOf := __src.chunkOf, handleGraftPrune := __src.handleGraftPrune,
  onIwantNode := __src.onIwantNode, decodeAndReencode := __src.decodeAndReencode, payloadOf := __src.payloadOf,
  chunkAt := __src.chunkAt, blockRoot := __src.blockRoot, shuffledNats := __src.shuffledNats, writing := __src.writing,
  verifyMerkleProof := __src.verifyMerkleProof }
definition Refine.Carry.bgFalse : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { gate := fun x => false, installed := fun x => false, got := fun x => false, stop := fun x => false,
    peerHas := fun x x_1 => false, offered := fun x x_1 => false, ledger := fun x x_1 => false,
    inWindow := fun x => false, retryArmed := fun x => false, lrArmed := fun x => false, parked := fun x x_1 => false,
    askedParked := fun x x_1 => false, promise := fun x x_1 => false, tied := fun x x_1 => false,
    made := fun x x_1 x_2 => false, stage := fun x x_1 x_2 x_3 => false, everLastResort := fun x => false,
    everEarly := fun x => false, everHeldBack := fun x => false, everTied := fun x => false,
    everSwitched := fun x => false, closedAsk := fun x => false }
definition Refine.Carry.bgHolders : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { gate := fun x => true, installed := fun x => true, got := fun x => true, stop := fun x => false,
    peerHas := fun x x_1 => false, offered := fun x x_1 => false, ledger := fun x x_1 => false,
    inWindow := fun x => false, retryArmed := fun x => false, lrArmed := fun x => false, parked := fun x x_1 => false,
    askedParked := fun x x_1 => false, promise := fun x x_1 => false, tied := fun x x_1 => false,
    made := fun x x_1 x_2 => false, stage := fun x x_1 x_2 x_3 => false, everLastResort := fun x => false,
    everEarly := fun x => false, everHeldBack := fun x => false, everTied := fun x => false,
    everSwitched := fun x => false, closedAsk := fun x => false }
definition Refine.Carry.cControl : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { w := Refine.Carry.stalledStart, ctx := Refine.Ask.ctx1, ans := Refine.Carry.ansExpired,
    bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord }
definition Refine.Carry.cControlAfter : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire 0 Refine.Ask.kA Refine.Ask.ctx1 Refine.Carry.ansExpired Refine.Carry.stalledStart,
    ctx := Refine.Ask.ctx1, ans := Refine.Carry.ansExpired, bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord }
definition Refine.Carry.cWitness : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { w := Refine.Carry.quietHeld, ctx := Refine.Ask.ctx1, ans := Refine.Carry.ansBlock,
    bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord }
definition Refine.Carry.cWitnessAfter : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire 0 Refine.Ask.kA Refine.Ask.ctx1 Refine.Carry.ansBlock Refine.Carry.quietHeld,
    ctx := Refine.Ask.ctx1, ans := Refine.Carry.ansBlock, bg := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord }
definition Refine.Carry.idleState : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer → Bool → Bool → Bool → (SpecTwin.Peer → Bool) → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord n₀ held stop armed off =>
  have __src := Refine.Carry.bgHolders kind ord;
  { gate := __src.gate, installed := __src.installed, got := fun n => if n = n₀ then held else true,
    stop := fun n => if n = n₀ then stop else false, peerHas := __src.peerHas,
    offered := fun n q => if n = n₀ then off q else false, ledger := __src.ledger, inWindow := __src.inWindow,
    retryArmed := fun n => if n = n₀ then armed else false, lrArmed := __src.lrArmed, parked := __src.parked,
    askedParked := __src.askedParked, promise := __src.promise, tied := __src.tied, made := __src.made,
    stage := __src.stage, everLastResort := __src.everLastResort, everEarly := __src.everEarly,
    everHeldBack := __src.everHeldBack, everTied := __src.everTied, everSwitched := __src.everSwitched,
    closedAsk := __src.closedAsk }
definition Refine.Carry.n0 : SpecTwin.Peer :=
0
definition Refine.Carry.parkedWorld : Nat →
  SpecTwin.Key → SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Ctx → Refine.Ask.World → Refine.Ask.World :=
fun i a p ctx w =>
  {
    st :=
      have __src := w.st;
      { askedAt := __src.askedAt, tried := __src.tried, offers := __src.offers, offered := __src.offered,
        promises := w.st.promises.erase (a, p),
        park := w.st.park.insert p ((w.st.park.getD p ∅).insert a.group (ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL)),
        askedParked := __src.askedParked, lastResortArmed := __src.lastResortArmed, ihaveFrames := __src.ihaveFrames,
        ihaveAsked := __src.ihaveAsked, heldBack := __src.heldBack, received := __src.received, mine := __src.mine,
        state := __src.state, tied := __src.tied, queue := __src.queue, pending := __src.pending,
        pendingIds := __src.pendingIds, tieSlot := __src.tieSlot, owed := __src.owed, owedAnswers := __src.owedAnswers,
        sentTo := __src.sentTo, slotHeld := __src.slotHeld, paceQueue := __src.paceQueue, stopPull := __src.stopPull,
        peerHas := __src.peerHas, holders := __src.holders, serveCount := __src.serveCount, origin := __src.origin,
        planned := __src.planned, pushed := __src.pushed, desc := __src.desc, inFlight := __src.inFlight,
        waiters := __src.waiters, sent := __src.sent, unwanted := __src.unwanted },
    timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
definition Refine.Carry.promState : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer → Bool → Bool → Bool → (SpecTwin.Peer → Bool) → (SpecTwin.Peer → Bool) → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord n₀ held stop armed off prom =>
  have __src := Refine.Carry.idleState kind ord n₀ held stop armed off;
  { gate := __src.gate, installed := __src.installed, got := __src.got, stop := __src.stop, peerHas := __src.peerHas,
    offered := __src.offered, ledger := __src.ledger, inWindow := __src.inWindow, retryArmed := __src.retryArmed,
    lrArmed := __src.lrArmed, parked := __src.parked, askedParked := __src.askedParked,
    promise := fun n q => if n = n₀ then prom q else false, tied := __src.tied, made := __src.made,
    stage := __src.stage, everLastResort := __src.everLastResort, everEarly := __src.everEarly,
    everHeldBack := __src.everHeldBack, everTied := __src.everTied, everSwitched := __src.everSwitched,
    closedAsk := __src.closedAsk }
definition Refine.Carry.promiseFire : Nat →
  SpecTwin.Key → SpecTwin.Peer → Float → SpecTwin.Ctx → Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.World → Refine.Ask.World :=
fun i a p at_ ctx ans w =>
  (Refine.Ask.run (SpecTwin.promiseDue a p at_) ctx ans
      { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }).snd
definition Refine.Carry.promisedOne : Refine.Ask.World :=
{ st := { offers := ∅.insert Refine.Ask.kA [1], promises := ∅.insert (Refine.Ask.kA, 1) 0.6 },
  timers := [(0.4, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue Refine.Ask.kA 1 0.6), (0.205, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer Refine.Ask.kA)] }
definition Refine.Carry.quietHeld : Refine.Ask.World :=
{ st := { offers := ∅.insert Refine.Ask.kA [1], mine := ∅.insert "g" 1 },
  timers := [(0.205, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer Refine.Ask.kA)] }
definition Refine.Carry.rOn : (kind ord : Type) → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord :=
fun kind ord =>
  { lastResort := true, owedAnswers := true, askLedger := true, heldBackRetry := true, rootTied := true,
    noReask := true, countsReset := true }
definition Refine.Carry.stalledStart : Refine.Ask.World :=
{ st := { offers := ∅.insert Refine.Ask.kA [1] }, timers := [(0.205, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer Refine.Ask.kA)] }
definition Refine.Carry.withBlocks : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer →
    SpecTwin.Chunk →
      Nat → SpecTwin.Root → Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord :=
fun kind ord n₀ k sl rt ans bg =>
  { gate := fun n => if n = n₀ then ans.authenticable k.fst else bg.gate n,
    installed := fun n => if n = n₀ then (ans.installedBlock sl rt).isSome else bg.installed n, got := bg.got,
    stop := bg.stop, peerHas := bg.peerHas, offered := bg.offered, ledger := bg.ledger, inWindow := bg.inWindow,
    retryArmed := bg.retryArmed, lrArmed := bg.lrArmed, parked := bg.parked, askedParked := bg.askedParked,
    promise := bg.promise, tied := bg.tied, made := bg.made, stage := bg.stage, everLastResort := bg.everLastResort,
    everEarly := bg.everEarly, everHeldBack := bg.everHeldBack, everTied := bg.everTied,
    everSwitched := bg.everSwitched, closedAsk := bg.closedAsk }
inductive Refine.Carry.Cfg : Type → Type → Type
constructor Refine.Carry.Cfg.mk : {kind ord : Type} →
  Refine.Ask.World → SpecTwin.Ctx → Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord
inductive Refine.Carry.Covered : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Chunk → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.Covered.asks : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord}
  {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a) →
      Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = false →
        Refine.Ask.stopP w.st a.group = false →
          ans.expired a.group = false →
            decide (ctx.now - (w.st.askedAt.get? a).getD SpecTwin.negInf < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW) = false →
              Refine.Ask.freeP ctx ans w.st a ≠ [] →
                (∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → b ∈ w.st.askedAt → b = a) →
                  (∀ (xs : List SpecTwin.Peer) (b : SpecTwin.Key), (ans.ownOrder xs b).Perm xs) →
                    ctx.now - ctx.now < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW →
                      Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                        { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.Covered.asksOther : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord}
  {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk ≠ k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a) →
      Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = false →
        Refine.Ask.stopP w.st a.group = false →
          ans.expired a.group = false →
            decide (ctx.now - (w.st.askedAt.get? a).getD SpecTwin.negInf < ctx.c.IWANT_WINDOW) = false →
              Refine.Ask.freeP ctx ans w.st a ≠ [] →
                Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                  { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.Covered.heldSame : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord}
  {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a) →
      Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = true →
        Refine.Ask.retryArmedP { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
              a.chunk =
            true →
          Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
            { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.Covered.heldLast : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord}
  {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.retryOffer a) →
      Refine.Ask.heldP w.st a.chunk = true →
        Refine.Ask.retryArmedP { st := w.st, timers := w.timers.eraseIdx i, made := w.made, crashed := w.crashed }
              a.chunk =
            false →
          Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st a.chunk = false →
            Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
              { w := Refine.Ask.retryFire i a ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
inductive Refine.Carry.CoveredRun : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Chunk → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.start : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  (c : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord), Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k c c
constructor Refine.Carry.CoveredRun.step : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {c₀ c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k c₀ c →
    Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k c c' → Refine.Carry.CoveredRun kind ord n₀ k c₀ c'
inductive Refine.Carry.FvInit : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → [DecidableEq ord] → [Inhabited ord] → [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.FvInit.mk : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord],
  (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve rd st) →
    (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.blocks rd st) →
      (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offer_holder rd st) →
        (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ledger_before rd st) →
          (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ask_holder rd st) →
            (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.claims_true rd st) →
              (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.window_retry rd st) →
                (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_offered rd st) →
                  (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_parked rd st) →
                    (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.after_gate rd st) →
                      (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.made_order rd st) →
                        (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.stage_made rd st) →
                          (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.asked_offered rd st) →
                            (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.first_ask rd st) →
                              (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.second_ask rd st) →
                                (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.two_before rd st) →
                                  (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.third_answered rd st) →
                                    (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.unparked_retry rd st) →
                                      (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.parked_timer rd st) →
                                        (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offer_active rd st) →
                                          (Refine.Carry.InitMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                              NoStallEquiv.asked_parked rd st) →
                                            Refine.Carry.FvInit kind ord

inductive Refine.Carry.FvRetryAsk : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → [DecidableEq ord] → [Inhabited ord] → [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.FvRetryAsk.mk : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord],
  (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve rd st) →
    (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.blocks rd st) →
      (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offer_holder rd st) →
        (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ledger_before rd st) →
          (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ask_holder rd st) →
            (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.claims_true rd st) →
              (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.window_retry rd st) →
                (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_offered rd st) →
                  (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_parked rd st) →
                    (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.after_gate rd st) →
                      (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.made_order rd st) →
                        (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.stage_made rd st) →
                          (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.asked_offered rd st) →
                            (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.first_ask rd st) →
                              (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.second_ask rd st) →
                                (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.two_before rd st) →
                                  (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.third_answered rd st) →
                                    (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                        NoStallEquiv.unparked_retry rd st) →
                                      (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                          NoStallEquiv.parked_timer rd st) →
                                        (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                            NoStallEquiv.offer_active rd st) →
                                          (Refine.Carry.RetryAskMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                              NoStallEquiv.asked_parked rd st) →
                                            Refine.Carry.FvRetryAsk kind ord
