  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] → [DecidableEq ord] → [Inhabited ord] → [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.FvRetryNoop.mk : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord],
  (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offers_resolve rd st) →
    (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.blocks rd st) →
      (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.offer_holder rd st) →
        (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ledger_before rd st) →
          (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.ask_holder rd st) →
            (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.claims_true rd st) →
              (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.window_retry rd st) →
                (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_offered rd st) →
                  (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.askedparked_parked rd st) →
                    (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.after_gate rd st) →
                      (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.made_order rd st) →
                        (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.stage_made rd st) →
                          (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.asked_offered rd st) →
                            (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.first_ask rd st) →
                              (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.second_ask rd st) →
                                (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st => NoStallEquiv.two_before rd st) →
                                  (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                      NoStallEquiv.third_answered rd st) →
                                    (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                        NoStallEquiv.unparked_retry rd st) →
                                      (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                          NoStallEquiv.parked_timer rd st) →
                                        (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                            NoStallEquiv.offer_active rd st) →
                                          (Refine.Carry.RetryNoopMeets kind ord fun rd st =>
                                              NoStallEquiv.asked_parked rd st) →
                                            Refine.Carry.FvRetryNoop kind ord
inductive Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises : SpecTwin.Ctx →
  Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.World → SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Chunk → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises.mk : ∀ {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
  {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk},
  Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st k = false →
    w.st.lastResortArmed.contains k = false →
      (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now w.st q k.fst = false) →
        (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), (w.st.askedParked.getD k ∅).contains q = false) →
          (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.tiedP w.st q k = false) →
            (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.queuedP w.st q k = false) →
              (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), w.made k q = 0) →
                Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st k n₀ = false → Refine.Carry.IdleButPromises ctx ans w n₀ k
inductive Refine.Carry.IdleW : SpecTwin.Ctx →
  Refine.Ask.Answers → Refine.Ask.World → SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Chunk → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.IdleW.mk : ∀ {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {w : Refine.Ask.World}
  {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk},
  Refine.Ask.windowP ctx w.st k = false →
    w.st.lastResortArmed.contains k = false →
      (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.parkedP ctx.now w.st q k.fst = false) →
        (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), (w.st.askedParked.getD k ∅).contains q = false) →
          (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.promisedP w.st q k = false) →
            (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.tiedP w.st q k = false) →
              (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), Refine.Ask.queuedP w.st q k = false) →
                (∀ (q : SpecTwin.Peer), w.made k q = 0) →
                  Refine.Ask.offeredP ans w.st k n₀ = false → Refine.Carry.IdleW ctx ans w n₀ k
inductive Refine.Carry.Meets : {m : Veil.Mode} →
  {ρ σ α : Type} → Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α → (ρ → Prop) → Veil.SProp ρ σ → Veil.SProp ρ σ → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.Meets.mk : ∀ {m : Veil.Mode} {ρ σ α : Type} {act : Veil.VeilM m ρ σ α} {assu : ρ → Prop}
  {pre post : Veil.SProp ρ σ},
  act.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming assu pre post → Refine.Carry.Meets act assu pre post
inductive Refine.Carry.PlainInv : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] → Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Ask.MState kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.PlainInv.mk : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {s : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord},
  (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
        ¬NoStallEquiv.work r s → s.installed N = true → s.offered N Q = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
    (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
        (s.installed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
          (s.got N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧
            (s.stop N = true → s.installed N = true) ∧ (s.tied N Q = true → ¬s.installed N = true)) →
      (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
          (s.offered N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
            (s.ledger N Q = true → s.got Q = true) ∧ (s.tied N Q = true → s.got Q = true)) →
        (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.ledger N Q = true → ¬s.gate N = true) →
          (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true → s.got Q = true) ∧
                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true → s.got Q = true)) →
            (∀ (Q N : SpecTwin.Peer), s.peerHas Q N = true → s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
              (∀ (N : SpecTwin.Peer), s.inWindow N = true → s.retryArmed N = true) →
                (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r → s.askedParked N Q = true → s.offered N Q = true) →
                  (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer), s.askedParked N Q = true → s.parked N Q = true) →
                    (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                        (s.offered N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                          (s.retryArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                            (s.lrArmed N = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                              (s.promise N Q = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                (s.made N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                      (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                        (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true → s.gate N = true) ∧
                                          (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true → s.gate N = true)) →
                      (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                          (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                              s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true) ∧
                            (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true) ∧
                              (s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o4 = true →
                                s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true)) →
                        (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                            (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true → s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                              (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askF N Q J = true → s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansQ Q N J = true → s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                  (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansOwed Q N J = true → s.made N Q J = true) ∧
                                    (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.ansF Q N J = true → s.made N Q J = true)) →
                          (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer) (J : ord),
                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                (s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true →
                                    s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true) ∧
                                  (s.promise N Q = true → s.offered N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true)) →
                            (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                  ¬s.installed N = true →
                                    s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o1 = true →
                                      (∃ J, s.stage NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass.askQ N Q J = true) ∨
                                        s.promise N Q = true ∨
                                          s.parked N Q = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true ∨ s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true) →
                              (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                  Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                    ¬s.installed N = true →
                                      s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o2 = true →
                                        s.askedParked N Q = true ∨ s.tied N Q = true) →
                                (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                    Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                      ¬s.installed N = true → ¬s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true) →
                                  (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                      Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                        s.made N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 = true →
                                          Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass.o3 ∨
                                            s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true ∨ s.peerHas Q N = true) →
                                    (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                        Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                          s.offered N Q = true →
                                            ¬s.parked N Q = true →
                                              ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                s.got N = true ∨ s.stop N = true ∨ s.retryArmed N = true) →
                                      (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                          Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                            s.offered N Q = true →
                                              s.parked N Q = true →
                                                ¬s.tied N Q = true →
                                                  ¬s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                    s.got N = true ∨
                                                      s.stop N = true ∨ s.retryArmed N = true ∨ s.lrArmed N = true) →
                                        (∀ (N M : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                            Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                              s.installed N = true →
                                                s.offered N M = true →
                                                  s.got N = true ∨
                                                    s.stop N = true ∨
                                                      s.retryArmed N = true ∨
                                                        s.lrArmed N = true ∨
                                                          (∃ Q J, Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q J) ∨
                                                            ∀ (Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                                              s.offered N Q = true →
                                                                s.parked N Q = true ∧ s.askedParked N Q = true) →
                                          (∀ (N Q : SpecTwin.Peer),
                                              Refine.Carry.AllOn kind ord r →
                                                s.askedParked N Q = true →
                                                  s.got N = true ∨
                                                    s.stop N = true ∨
                                                      s.tied N Q = true ∨
                                                        s.peerHas Q N = true ∨
                                                          ∃ J, Refine.Carry.PipelineP kind ord s N Q J) →
                                            Refine.Carry.PlainInv kind ord r s
inductive Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered : (kind ord : Type) →
  SpecTwin.Peer → SpecTwin.Chunk → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.gone : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
  {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
    w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = none →
      Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
        { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.other : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
  {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
    w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t →
      (t != at_) = true →
        Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
          { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.otherGroup : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
    w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t →
      (t != at_) = false →
        a.group ≠ k.fst →
          Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
            { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.due : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
  {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
      w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t →
        (t != at_) = false →
          (∀ (b : SpecTwin.Key), b.chunk = a.chunk → (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises → b = a) →
            ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL →
              Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.more : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {i : Nat}
  {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx} {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers}
  {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk = k →
    w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
      w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t →
        (t != at_) = false →
          (∃ b, b ≠ a ∧ b.chunk = a.chunk ∧ (b, p) ∈ w.st.promises) →
            ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL →
              Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
constructor Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered.otherChunk : ∀ {kind ord : Type} {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk}
  {i : Nat} {d : Float} {a : SpecTwin.Key} {p : SpecTwin.Peer} {at_ t : Float} {ctx : SpecTwin.Ctx}
  {ans : Refine.Ask.Answers} {bg : Refine.Ask.MState kind ord} {w : Refine.Ask.World},
  a.chunk ≠ k →
    a.group = k.fst →
      w.timers[i]? = some (d, SpecTwin.Timer.promiseDue a p at_) →
        w.st.promises.get? (a, p) = some t →
          (t != at_) = false →
            ctx.now < ctx.now + ctx.c.PARK_TTL →
              Refine.Carry.PromiseCovered kind ord n₀ k { w := w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
                { w := Refine.Carry.promiseFire i a p at_ ctx ans w, ctx := ctx, ans := ans, bg := bg }
inductive Refine.Carry.Run : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              SpecTwin.Peer →
                SpecTwin.Chunk →
                  Nat →
                    SpecTwin.Root →
                      Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.Run.start : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} (c : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord), Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c
constructor Refine.Carry.Run.step : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {c₀ c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c₀ c →
    Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c' → Refine.Carry.Run kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c₀ c'
inductive Refine.Carry.Step : (kind ord : Type) →
  [DecidableEq kind] →
    [Inhabited kind] →
      [NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] →
        [DecidableEq ord] →
          [Inhabited ord] →
            [NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] →
              SpecTwin.Peer →
                SpecTwin.Chunk →
                  Nat →
                    SpecTwin.Root →
                      Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord → Prop
constructor Refine.Carry.Step.covered : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.Covered kind ord n₀ k c c' → Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'
constructor Refine.Carry.Step.same : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}
  {r : Refine.Ask.MTheory kind ord} {c c' : Refine.Carry.Cfg kind ord},
  Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c' = Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c →
    Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'
constructor Refine.Carry.Step.other : ∀ {kind ord : Type} [inst : DecidableEq kind] [inst_1 : Inhabited kind]
  [inst_2 : NoStallEquiv.kind_EnumClass kind] [inst_3 : DecidableEq ord] [inst_4 : Inhabited ord]
  [inst_5 : NoStallEquiv.ord_EnumClass ord] {n₀ : SpecTwin.Peer} {k : SpecTwin.Chunk} {sl : Nat} {rt : SpecTwin.Root}

  Refine.Carry.MInv kind ord r (Refine.Carry.abs kind ord n₀ k sl rt c') → Refine.Carry.Step kind ord n₀ k sl rt r c c'

  [node_inhabited : Inhabited node] (χ : BridgeToy.State.Label → Type)
  [χ_rep :
    (__veil_f : BridgeToy.State.Label) →
      Veil.FieldRepresentation (BridgeToy.State.Label.toDomain node __veil_f)

      Veil.LawfulFieldRepresentation (BridgeToy.State.Label.toDomain node __veil_f)
        (BridgeToy.State.Label.toCodomain node __veil_f) (χ __veil_f) (χ_rep __veil_f)]

    (BridgeToy.Invariants ρ σ node χ)
